# Feature parity ledger

Statuses use the required vocabulary: `未调查`, `已调查`, `实现中`, `已实现但未验证`, `已验证`, or `受外部条件阻塞`. A source project inventory is not proof that each behavior has been audited. Rows below are initial traceable scopes and will be split further as source branches and registrations are examined. On 2026-10-08, 46 legacy rows were aligned by inserting an empty `Configuration and defaults` cell; all existing values and statuses were preserved. An empty cell records missing ledger content, not an assertion that the feature has no configuration.

## Bounded generated-class fallback evidence (open ledger rows)

SER-TT-ABSENT-001 and SER-ASSET-FACTORY-001 remain open. This increment integrates two source-probed generated-class layouts from Unity 2019.4.40f1 with empty file TypeTrees: TextAsset (class 49) and MonoScript (class 115). ObjectWorkspace decodes both through their TPK layouts and the reference graph scans both completely. TextAsset PathID 42 yields m_Name=Hero and m_Script=print(1), then exports as a class-49 YAML asset through export-project. MonoScript PathID 42 yields HeroController, the VRC.SDK namespace, Assembly-CSharp, and execution order 200. A fresh isolated source probe matched all four SF_HEX/SF_READ rows for each case against the committed fixture.

The integrated regressions are object_workspace_recovers_source_generated_textasset_without_usable_file_typetree, object_workspace_recovers_source_generated_monoscript_without_usable_file_typetree, object_workspace_keeps_unknown_no_tree_objects_unresolved, export_project_recovers_generated_textasset_without_usable_file_typetree, and export_project_refuses_unknown_object_without_a_usable_type_tree. The unknown-class case remains undecoded and prevents project export before output creation. Other classes and versions, TPK TypeTreeObject materialization, MonoBehaviour/assembly recovery, and the complete factory branch set remain open.
